================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.SALDO.grp_operacao
* Classe: commandgroup (Base: commandgroup)
* Registro: 58
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE cmd_consulta.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE cmd_consulta.Click
ThisForm.Operacao = "CONSULTA"

ThisForm.Cliente = CrSigMvSlc.Contas
LcCliente = ThisForm.Cliente
ThisForm.poDataMgr.Requery('CrSigCdCli')
Select CrSigCdCli
Go Top

thisform.page.hist.get_grupo.value   = thisform.page.saldo.get_grupo.value
thisform.page.hist.get_moeda.value   = CrSigMvSlc.moedas && thisform.page.saldo.grd_Saldo.column3.text1.value && 
thisform.page.hist.get_hconta.value  = CrSigMvSlc.contas && thisform.page.saldo.grd_Saldo.column1.text1.value && 
thisform.page.hist.get_hdconta.value = CrSigMvSlc.RClis  && thisform.page.saldo.grd_Saldo.column2.text1.value && 

thisform.lockscreen = .t.

thisform.page.hist.get_grupo.refresh
thisform.page.hist.get_moeda.refresh
thisform.page.hist.get_hconta.refresh
thisform.page.hist.get_hdconta.refresh

If empty(thisform.page.hist.get_hconta.value)
	messagebox('Nenhuma Conta/Saldo foi escolhida para consulta !',64,'')
	thisform.page.activepage = 1
	thisform.page.saldo.get_grupo.setfocus
	thisform.page.refresh
	thisform.lockscreen = .f.
	return(.t.)
Endif

lcGSccr = crSigMvSlc.Grupos
lcCSccr = CrSigMvSlc.Contas
lcMSccr = CrSigMvSlc.Moedas

Create Cursor crMatrizes (Grupos c(10), Contas c(10), Moedas c(3))
Insert Into crMatrizes (Grupos, Contas, Moedas) Values (lcGSccr, lcCSccr, lcMSccr)

llDatai = ThisForm.Page.Saldo.Dt_inicial.Value
llDataf = ThisForm.Page.Saldo.Dt_Final.Value
llDatai = Evaluate([{^]+TransForm((DtoS(Max(llDatai,CtoD('01/01/1900')))+'000000'),'@R 9999-99-99 99:99:99')+[}])
llDataf = Evaluate([{^]+TransForm((DtoS(Max(llDataf,CtoD('01/01/1900')))+'235959'),'@R 9999-99-99 99:99:99')+[}])

If Used('CrSigMvCcr')
	ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.RecordSource = ''
	Use In CrSigMvCcr
EndIf

ThisForm.Matrizes = (crSigMvSlc.Matriz <> [ ]) And ;
		(MessageBox([Esta Conta Pertence a Um Grupo de Cobrança.] + Chr(13) + ;
					[Deseja Processar Todas Contas do Grupo?], 4+32+256, ;
					[Empresa ] + Iif(crSigMvSlc.Matriz = [M], [Matriz], [Filial])) = 6)

If ThisForm.Matrizes
	If (crSigMvSlc.Matriz = [M])
		lcQuery = [Select IClis, Grupos From SigCdCli Where ContaMats = '] + CrSigMvSlc.Contas + [' And IClis <> '] + CrSigMvSlc.Contas + [']
	Else
		lcQuery = [Select IClis, Grupos From SigCdCli Where ContaMats = '] + CrSigMvSlc.ContaMats + [' And IClis <> '] + CrSigMvSlc.Contas + [']
	EndIf
	
	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpMat') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpMat)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	Select crTmpMat
	Scan
		Insert Into crMatrizes (Grupos, Contas, Moedas) Values (crTmpMat.Grupos, crTmpMat.IClis, lcMSccr)
	EndScan
	Use In crTmpMat
EndIf

lcQuery = [Select ' ' as Tipo, a.Saldos as SaldosP, a.*, 0 as Seleciona From SigMvCcr a ] + ;
		   [Where a.GruConMoes = '] + Replicate(Chr(254), 23) + ['] 

If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpMccr') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpMccr)')
	Return 0
EndIf

Select crMatrizes
Scan
	lcGCM = crMatrizes.Grupos + crMatrizes.Contas + crMatrizes.Moedas
	
	lcQuery = [Select 'R' as Tipo, a.Saldos as SaldosP, a.*, 0 as Seleciona From SigMvCcr a ] + ;
			   [Where a.GruConMoes = '] + lcGCM + [' And ] + ;
			         [a.Datas Between ?llDatai And ?llDataf] 

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpMcc1') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpMcc1)')
		Return 0
	EndIf

	lcQuery = [Select 'P' as Tipo, b.Saldos as SaldosP, b.* From SigMvCpv b ] + ;
			   [Where b.GruConMoes = '] + lcGCM + [' And ] + ;
			         [b.Datas Between ?llDatai And ?llDataf]

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpMcpv') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpMccr)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	
	Go Top In crTmpMccr
	
	Select CrTmpMcc1
	Scan
		Scatter Memvar Memo
		Insert Into crTmpMccr From Memvar
	EndScan
	
	Select CrTmpMcpv
	Scan
		Scatter Memvar Memo
		Insert Into crTmpMccr From Memvar
	EndScan
EndScan

Go Top In crTmpMccr

Select *, .f. as Prorrogados From CrTmpMccr Into Cursor TMccr ;
Order By Grupos,Contas,Moedas,datas,nopers

Select tMccr
Use Dbf('tMccr') In 0 Alias CrSigMvCcr Again
Use In tMccr

* Richard em 14/02/2012 - Para ordenar certo quando houver matriz e filiais processadas
If ThisForm.Matrizes
	lcMat = Padr(Iif(crSigMvSlc.Matriz = [M], CrSigMvSlc.Contas, CrSigMvSlc.ContaMats), 10)
	Update crSigMvCcr Set Contas = lcMat, GruConMoes = crSigMvCcr.Grupos + lcMat + crSigMvCcr.Moedas
EndIf

* Richard em 03/04/2012 - Para marcar títulos Prorrogados
Select crSigMvCcr
Scan
	lcQuery = [Select * From SigOpMvv Where EmpDopNcs = '] + crSigMvCcr.EmpDopNcs + [']
	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalOpMvv') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalOpMvv)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	If (Reccount([LocalOpMvv]) > 0)
		Replace Prorrogados With .t. In crSigMvCcr
	ENDIF
	replace seleciona with 0  In crSigMvCcr
EndScan

Select CrSigMvCcr
*!*	Index on GruConMoes + CidChaves Tag GruConMoe For Tipo = [R]
*!*	Index on GruConMoes + Dtos(DataConcs) + CidChaves Tag GruConMoec For Concs
*!*	Index on GruConMoes + CidChaves Tag GruConMoen For !Concs
*!*	Index on GruConMoes + CidChaves Tag GruConMoeP
Index on GruConMoes + Dtos(Datas) + CidChaves Tag GruConMoe For Tipo = [R]
Index on GruConMoes + Dtos(DataConcs) + CidChaves Tag GruConMoec For Concs
Index on GruConMoes + Dtos(Datas) + CidChaves Tag GruConMoen For !Concs
Index on GruConMoes + Dtos(Datas) + CidChaves Tag GruConMoeP
* Richard em 15/10/2012 - Tem cIdChaves que fica fora de ordem
Set Order to GruConMoe
Go Bottom

If Reccount() > 0
	If ThisForm.Matrizes
		Select CrSigMvCcr
		Go Top In CrSigMvCcr
		lnSal = 0
		Select CrSigMvCcr
		Scan
			If (CrSigMvCcr.Opers = [D])
				lnSal = lnSal + Abs(CrSigMvCcr.Valors)
			Else
				lnSal = lnSal - Abs(CrSigMvCcr.Valors)
			EndIf
			
			Replace Saldos With lnSal
		EndScan
	EndIf
	
	With ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento
		.RecordSource = "CrSigMvCcr"
		.Col_data.ControlSource = "Datas"
		.Col_saldo.ControlSource = "Saldos"
		.Col_hist.ControlSource = "Hists"
		.col_valor.ControlSource = "Valors"
		.col_oper.ControlSource = "Alltrim(Iif(CrSigMvCcr.Opers=[D], ThisForm.TxtDeb, ThisForm.TxtCre))"
		.col_emp.ControlSource = "iif(!Empty(CrSigMvCcr.EmpCCs),CrSigMvCcr.EmpCcs,CrSigMvCcr.Emps)" && Tiago - 22/02/2016 - Se tiver empresa no EMPCCS mostra ela
		.Col_Saldo.InputMask = '99,999,999,999.99'
		.Col_Saldo.Text1.InputMask = '99,999,999,999.99'
		.Column1.ControlSource = "Docus"
		.Column2.ControlSource = "NFs"
		.Column3.ControlSource = "Titulos"
		.Column4.ControlSource = "Tipo"
		.Col_selec.ControlSource = "Seleciona"
		.SetAll('DynamicForeColor',;
		'Iif(CrSigMvCcr.TitCancs=1,RGB(255,0,0),Iif(CrSigMvCcr.Concs,RGB(0,0,255),Iif(CrSigMvCcr.Pagos=[0],RGB(0,128,0),Iif(CrSigMvCcr.Prorrogados,RGB(255,128,0),RGB(0,0,0)))))','Column')
								     * 255,0,0 => Vermelho  //           0,0,255 => Azul  //                   0,128,0 => Verde Escuro  //             255,128,0 => Laranja    // 0,0,0 => Preto
		.SetAll('DynamicBackColor','Iif(crSigMvCcr.Tipo=[P], Rgb(245,251,136), Iif(!IsEmpty(CrSigMvCcr.DtAudits), RGB(220,255,220), RGB(255,255,255)))','Column') 
									* 245,251,136 => Amarelo  //  220,255,220 => Verde Claro  //  255,255,255 => Branco
	EndWith

	If fChecaAcesso( "SIGOPIND", "CONCILIA" ) And CrSigCdCli.Concilias = 1
		With ThisForm.Page.Hist
			.Chk_Concilia.Value   = ( CrSigMvCcr.Concs )
			.Chk_Concilia.Enabled = ( CrSigCdCli.Concilias = 1 )
			.Chk_Auditoria.Value  = !IsEmpty( CrSigMvCcr.DtAudits )
		EndWith
	EndIf

	thisform.page.hist.grd_lancamento.refresh
	thisform.page.hist.grd_lancamento.setfocus

	With thisForm.Page.Hist.Cmb_Concilia
		.clear
		.additem('Lançamentos Realizados')
		.additem('Só Conciliados')
		.additem('Não Conciliados')
		.additem('Saldo Diário - Todos')
		.additem('Saldo Diário - Conciliados')
		.additem('Saldo Diário - Não Conciliados')
		.additem('Realizado e Previsão')
		.value = 'Lançamentos Realizados'
	Endwith
	ThisForm.Page.Hist.Enabled = .t.
	thisform.page.activepage = 2
	thisform.lockscreen = .f.
	thisform.page.hist.getTotal.Value = 0.00	&& mena 13/11/2017 - limpa campo de total 
Else
	Messagebox('Nenhum Registro Selecionado. Verifique Periodo Informado...',0+48,'')
	Thisform.lockscreen = .f.
	Thisform.Page.Saldo.Grd_saldo.Column1.SetFocus
ENDIF

SELECT crSigmvCcr
GO bottom

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE cmd_sair.Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE cmd_sair.Click
ThisForm.Release


ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.grd_lancamento
* Classe: grid (Base: grid)
* Registro: 60
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE AfterRowColChange
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE AfterRowColChange
Lparameters nColIndex

Local lnLanCombo, ldDtCnc, laCampo1(3,2), laCampo2(2,2), laCampo3(2,2), laCampo4(2,2), laCampo5(1,2)
Local lcEmpr, lcDope, lnNume, lnNumc, lnVoper, lnNoper
Private lcDopc

ThisForm.DesabContainer()

lcCliente = ThisForm.Cliente

If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.Requery('CrSigCdCli') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (AfterRowColChange - crSigCdCli)')
	Return 0
EndIf

Select CrSigCdCli
Go Top

If Not Empty(This.RecordSource)
	Select (This.RecordSource)
EndIf

lnLanCombo = Iif(This.Parent.Cmb_Concilia.Enabled,This.Parent.Cmb_Concilia.ListIndex, 1)

With ThisForm.page.hist 
	If InList(lnLanCombo,1)  &&1,2,3
		.Chk_Concilia.Value   = CrSigMvCcr.Concs
		.chk_concilia.Enabled = (CrSigCdCli.Concilias = 1)
		.Chk_Auditoria.Value  = !IsEmpty(CrSigMvCcr.DtAudits)
	Else
		If InList(lnLanCombo,2,3)  &&1,2,3
			.Chk_Concilia.Value    = CrSigMvCcr.Concs
			.chk_concilia.Enabled  = (CrSigCdCli.Concilias = 1)
			.Chk_Auditoria.Enabled = .T.
			.Chk_Auditoria.Value   = !IsEmpty(CrSigMvCcr.DtAudits)
		Else
			.chk_concilia.Enabled  = .F.
			.Chk_Auditoria.Enabled = .F.
		Endif
	EndIf
	.Chk_Quitação.Enabled = .f.
	.chk_Follow.Enabled = ( CrSigCdCli.Concilias = 1 )
EndWith

ldDtCnc = iif(IsEmpty(ThisForm.DataConcilia), Datetime(), ThisForm.DataConcilia)
This.Parent.Get_Data.Value = Iif(IsEmpty(CrSigMvCcr.DataConcs), ldDtCnc, CrSigMvCcr.DataConcs)
This.Parent.Get_Hist.Refresh
This.Parent.Get_Hist2.Refresh
This.Parent.Get1.Refresh
This.Parent.Get3.Refresh
This.Parent.Get_DtAudits.Value = Nvl(CrSigMvCcr.DtAudits,Ctod('//'))
This.Parent.Get_DtAudits.Refresh
This.Parent.Get_Auditors.Refresh
This.Parent.Get_Data.Refresh

_MAnexa = .f.
lcEmpr = crSigMvCcr.Emps
lcDope = crSigMvCcr.Dopes
lnNume = crSigMvCcr.Numes
lcDopc = crSigMvCcr.Dopcs
lnNumc = crSigMvCcr.Numcs
lnVoper= crSigMvCcr.VOpers
lnOper = crSigMvCcr.Nopers
lcEmpo = Left( CrSigMvCcr.VOpers, 3 ) 
lcvope = Val(Padl( Alltrim( Substr( crSigMvCcr.Vopers, 4 ) ), 7 ))
lcJob = crSigMvCcr.Jobs

lcQuery = [Select NDopes, RealPrevs ] + ;
		    [From SigOpOpe ] + ;
		   [Where Dopes = '] + lcDopc + [']

If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crSigOpOpe') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crSigOpOpe)')
	Return 0
EndIf

lcQuery = [Select cIdChaves ] + ;
		    [From SigCdPgr ] + ;
		   [Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcEmpr + lcDope + Str(lnNume, 6) + [']

If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crAchou') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crAchou 1)')
	Return 0
EndIf

If (Reccount('crAchou') > 0)
	_MAnexa = .t.
EndIf

If Not _MAnexa
	lcQuery = [Select cIdChaves ] + ;
			    [From SigMvCab ] + ;
			   [Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcEmpr + lcDope + Str(lnNume, 6) + [']

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crAchou') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crAchou 2)')
		Return 0
	EndIf

	If (Reccount('crAchou') > 0)
		_MAnexa = .t.
	EndIf
EndIf

If Not _MAnexa
	If (fChecaAcesso('C' + Padl(crSigOpOpe.NDopes, 8, '0')) And ;
	    Not Empty(crSigMvCcr.Emps) And Not Empty(crSigMvCcr.Dopcs) And Not Empty(crSigMvCcr.Numcs))
		_MAnexa = .t.
	EndIf
EndIf

If Not _MAnexa
	lcQuery = [Select cIdChaves ] + ;
			    [From SigCqChm ] + ;
			   [Where Emps = '] + lcEmpo + [' And ] + ;
			   		 [Numos = ] + Str(lcvope, 8)

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crAchou') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crAchou 3)')
		Return 0
	EndIf

	If (Reccount('crAchou') > 0)
		_MAnexa = .t.
	EndIf
EndIf

If Not _MAnexa
	lcQuery = [Select cIdChaves ] + ;
			    [From SigPrCtb ] + ;
			   [Where EmpDopNums = '] + lcEmpr + lcDope + Str(lnNume, 6) + [']

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crAchou') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crAchou 4)')
		Return 0
	EndIf

	If (Reccount('crAchou') > 0)
		_MAnexa = .t.
	EndIf
EndIf

If Not _MAnexa
	lcQuery = [Select cIdChaves ] + ;
			    [From SigCdNec ] + ;
			   [Where EmpDnPs = '] + lcEmpr + lcDope + Str(lnNume, 10) + [']

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crAchou') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crAchou 6)')
		Return 0
	EndIf

	If (Reccount('crAchou') > 0)
		_MAnexa = .t.
	EndIf
EndIf

laCampo3(1,1) = 'Empos'
laCampo3(1,2) = lcEmpr
laCampo3(2,1) = 'Nopers'
laCampo3(2,2) = lnOper

laCampo4(1,1) = 'Empos'
laCampo4(1,2) = lcEmpr
laCampo4(2,1) = 'Nopercs'
laCampo4(2,2) = lnOper

lcQuery = [Select cIdChaves ] + ;
		    [From SigCdPbx ] + ;
		   [Where Nopers = ] + Str(lnOper, 7) + [ And ] + ;
		   		 [EmpOs = '] + lcEmpr + [']

If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crAchou') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crAchou 5)')
	Return 0
EndIf

If (Reccount('crAchou') > 0)
	ThisForm.Page.Hist.Chk_Quitação.Enabled = .t.
Else
	lcQuery = [Select cIdChaves ] + ;
			    [From SigCdPbx ] + ;
			   [Where Nopercs = ] + Str(lnOper, 7) + [ And ] + ;
			   		 [EmpOs = '] + lcEmpr + [']

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crAchou') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crAchou 5)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	If (Reccount('crAchou') > 0)
		ThisForm.Page.Hist.Chk_Quitação.Enabled = .t.
	EndIf
EndIf
ThisForm.Page.Hist.Anexa.Enabled = _MAnexa
ThisForm.Page.Hist.Chk_follow.Enabled = .f.

lcSql = [Select * From SigCqOch Where Nopers = ]+Str(crSigMvCcr.Nopers)
If ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'CrAchou') > 0 And Reccount('CrAchou') > 0
	ThisForm.Page.Hist.Chk_follow.Enabled = .t.
EndIf
Select CrSigMvCcr


This.Parent.getCtaCob.Value = []
This.Parent.getNomCob.Value = []

lcWher  = [Select a.Contas, b.RClis ] + ;
			[From SigMvCcr a, SigCdCli b ] + ;
		   [Where a.cIdChaves = '] + crSigMvCcr.cIdChaves + [' And ] + ;
		   		 [a.Contas = b.IClis]

lnErro  = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'LocalCta')
If lnErro < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return 0
EndIf
Go Top In LocalCta
If Not Eof([LocalCta])
	This.Parent.getCtaCob.Value = LocalCta.Contas
	This.Parent.getNomCob.Value = LocalCta.RClis
EndIf
This.Parent.getCtaCob.Refresh 
This.Parent.getNomCob.Refresh 

This.Parent.getdjob.Value = []
lcWher  = [Select RClis From SigCdCli Where Iclis = '] + lcJob + [']
lnErro  = ThisForm.Podatamgr.SqlExecute(lcWher,'LocalCta')
If lnErro < 1
	MessageBox('Favor reinicializar o processo.',16,'Falha na Conexão')
	Return 0
EndIf
Go Top In LocalCta
If Not Eof([LocalCta])
	This.Parent.getdjob.Value = LocalCta.RClis
EndIf
This.Parent.getjob.Refresh 
This.Parent.getdjob.Refresh 


ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.grd_lancamento.Col_Data.Text1
* Classe: textbox (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 62
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE KeyPress
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE KeyPress
LPARAMETERS nKeyCode, nShiftAltCtrl

Local loGrupo, loPagina
loGrupo = Thisform.Page.Hist

Do Case

	Case nKeyCode = -2
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.Cmd_Alterar.Setfocus
		loGrupo.Cmd_Alterar.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = -3 
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.Cmd_Excluir.SetFocus
		loGrupo.Cmd_Excluir.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = -4 
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.Procura.SetFocus
		loGrupo.Procura.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = -5 
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.Cmd_Impressao.SetFocus
		loGrupo.Cmd_Impressao.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = -1
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.followup.SetFocus
		loGrupo.followup.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = -6 
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.anexa.SetFocus
		loGrupo.anexa.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = -8 
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.chk_quitação.SetFocus
		loGrupo.chk_quitação.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = -7
		NoDefault
		If loGrupo.Chk_Concilia.Value
			loGrupo.Chk_Concilia.Value = .f.
		Else
			loGrupo.Chk_Concilia.Value = .t.
		Endif
		loGrupo.chk_concilia.SetFocus
		loGrupo.chk_concilia.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = 133
		NoDefault
		If loGrupo.Chk_Auditoria.Value
			loGrupo.Chk_Auditoria.Value = .f.
		Else
			loGrupo.Chk_Auditoria.Value = .t.
		Endif
		loGrupo.chk_auditoria.SetFocus
		loGrupo.chk_auditoria.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = 13 
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.Cmd_consulta.SetFocus
		loGrupo.Cmd_consulta.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = 95
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.followupI.SetFocus
		loGrupo.followupI.Click()

Endcase

ENDPROC

* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.grd_lancamento.Col_Oper.Text1

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE KeyPress

PROCEDURE KeyPress
LPARAMETERS nKeyCode, nShiftAltCtrl

Local loGrupo, loPagina
loGrupo = Thisform.Page.Hist

Do Case

	Case nKeyCode = -2
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.Cmd_Alterar.Setfocus
		loGrupo.Cmd_Alterar.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = -3 
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.Cmd_Excluir.SetFocus
		loGrupo.Cmd_Excluir.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = -4 
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.Procura.SetFocus
		loGrupo.Procura.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = -5 
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.Cmd_Impressao.SetFocus
		loGrupo.Cmd_Impressao.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = -1 
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.followup.SetFocus
		loGrupo.followup.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = -6 
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.anexa.SetFocus
		loGrupo.anexa.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = -8 
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.chk_quitação.SetFocus
		loGrupo.chk_quitação.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = -7
		NoDefault
		If loGrupo.Chk_Concilia.Value
			loGrupo.Chk_Concilia.Value = .f.
		Else
			loGrupo.Chk_Concilia.Value = .t.
		Endif
		loGrupo.chk_concilia.SetFocus
		loGrupo.chk_concilia.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = 133
		NoDefault
		If loGrupo.Chk_Auditoria.Value
			loGrupo.Chk_Auditoria.Value = .f.
		Else
			loGrupo.Chk_Auditoria.Value = .t.
		Endif
		loGrupo.chk_auditoria.SetFocus
		loGrupo.chk_auditoria.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = 13 
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.Cmd_consulta.SetFocus
		loGrupo.Cmd_consulta.Click()

	Case nKeyCode = 95
		NoDefault
		loGrupo.followupI.SetFocus
		loGrupo.followupI.Click()

Endcase

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.grd_lancamento.col_selec.Header1
* Classe: header (Base: header)
* Registro: 81
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
If (ThisForm.page.hIST.grd_lancamento.col_selec.Header1.Tag = [X])
	ThisForm.page.hIST.grd_lancamento.col_selec.Header1.Tag = []
	Replace All seleciona With 0 In CrSigMvCcr
Else
	ThisForm.page.hIST.grd_lancamento.col_selec.Header1.Tag = [X]
	Replace All seleciona With 1 In CrSigMvCcr
EndIf
ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.grd_lancamento.col_selec.Check1
* Classe: checkbox (Base: checkbox)
* Registro: 82
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
LOCAL lnReg, lnSel, lcCur

lnSel=SELECT()

*** Mena - 13/11/2017 - foi criada uma coluna para selecionar as linhas e fazer a soma ***
this.Refresh
*** Mena - 12/12/2017 - ajustado para prever cursores diferentes na mesma grade ***
lcCur = ALLTRIM(ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.RecordSource)

SELECT (lcCur)
lnReg = RECNO(lcCur)
LOCATE for .f.
GO lnReg

IF this.Value = 1
	replace seleciona with 1 in (lcCur)
ELSE
	replace seleciona with 0 in (lcCur)
ENDIF
IF UPPER(lcCur) = 'CRSIGMVCCR'
	thisform.calcsaldo() 
ENDIF
SELECT (lcCur)
GO lnReg

SELECT (lnSel)

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.Cmd_Excluir
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 83
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Local llsaida

If This.Enabled And Not (crSigMvCcr.Tipo = [P])
	Select CrSigMvCcr

	ThisForm.DesabContainer()

	If !This.Visible
		Return
	Endif

	If !Eof()

		pEmp   = crSigMvCcr.Emps
		pNoper = crSigMvCcr.Nopers
		lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.Requery('CrAltcc')
		Select crAltcc
		Go Top
		Ok = .t.
		DO While !Eof() And Ok
			If CrAltcc.Opers # CrSigMvCcr.Opers
				lcComple = '( Contra Partida )'
			Else
				lcComple = ' '
			Endif
			okdt = fVerificaBloqueio(crAltcc.datas, ThisForm.poDatamgr)=0
			if !okdt
				messagebox('Periodo Bloqueado !!!',0+48,'')
				ok=.f.
			EndIf
			okdt = fBloqueioPorPeriodo(crAltcc.datas, ThisForm.poDatamgr)=0
			if !okdt
				messagebox('Periodo Bloqueado !!!',0+48,'')
				ok=.f.
			endif
			If crAltcc.autos and ok
				messagebox('Operação Inválida !! Movimentação Automática !'+lccomple,0+48,'')
				ok=.f.
			endif
			If crAltcc.Concs and ok
				messagebox('Operação Inválida !! Movimentação Conciliada !'+lccomple,0+48,'')
				ok=.f.
			endif
			If !fAcessoContab( Usuar, 'C', crAltcc.SGrupos ) and ok
				Messagebox( 'Acesso ao grupo Negado...', 32, '' )
				ok=.f.
			Endif
			If !fAcessoContas( Usuar, crAltcc.SGrupos, 'C', crAltcc.SContas ) and ok
				Messagebox( 'Acesso a conta Negado...', 32, '' )
				ok=.f.
			Endif
			If crAltcc.ContaPgs = 1 And crAltcc.ValPags # 0 and ok
				messagebox('Operação Inválida !! Movimentação já foi Baixada!'+lccomple,0+48,'')
				ok = .f.
			EndIf
			Select CrAltcc
			Skip
		EndDo
			
		Select crAltcc
		Go Top

		If ok And !Eof()
			Scatter Memvar Memo
			Do Form SigOpAcc With ThisForm,"EXCLUIR"
			This.Parent.Cmb_Concilia.InteractiveChange
		Endif
		Select CrSigMvCcr
		Locate For Nopers = pNoper
		ThisForm.Page.Saldo.Grd_Saldo.Refresh
	EndIf
	ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.Refresh
	ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.columns(1).SetFocus
EndIf

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.Anexa
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 84
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Local lcEmpr, lcDope, lnNume, lnNumc, lnVoper, lnOper, lcEmpo, lcVope
Local laCampo1(3,2), laCampo2(2,2), laCampo3(1,2)
Private lcDopc

If This.Enabled And Not (crSigMvCcr.Tipo = [P])
	ThisForm.DesabContainer()

	_MAnexa = .f.
	lcEmpr = crSigMvCcr.Emps
	lcDope = crSigMvCcr.Dopes
	lnNume = crSigMvCcr.Numes
	lcDopc = crSigMvCcr.Dopcs
	lnNumc = crSigMvCcr.Numcs
	lnVoper= crSigMvCcr.VOpers
	lnOper = crSigMvCcr.Nopers
	lcEmpo = Left( CrSigMvCcr.VOpers, 3 ) 
	lcvope = Val(Padl( Alltrim( Substr( crSigMvCcr.Vopers, 4 ) ), 7 ))

	laCampo1(1,1) = 'Emps'
	laCampo1(1,2) = lcEmpr
	laCampo1(2,1) = 'Dopes'
	laCampo1(2,2) = lcDope
	laCampo1(3,1) = 'Numes'
	laCampo1(3,2) = lnNume

	laCampo2(1,1) = 'Emps'
	laCampo2(1,2) = lcEmpo
	laCampo2(2,1) = 'Numos'
	laCampo2(2,2) = lcvope

	laCampo3(1,1) = 'EmpDopNums'
	laCampo3(1,2) = lcEmpr + lcdope + Str(lnNume,6)


	lcQuery = [Select NDopes, RealPrevs From SigOpOpe Where Dopes = ?lcDopc ]
	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crSigOpOpe') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crSigOpOpe)')
		Return 0
	EndIf

	Select crSigMvCcr
	If Not Eof()
		Do Case
			Case ThisForm.poDataMgr.ChkRegister('SigCdPgr',@laCampo1)
				Do Form SigCdPgr With Left( CrSigMvCcr.Dopes, 1 ), .t., CrSigMvCcr.Emps, CrSigMvCcr.Dopes, CrSigMvCcr.Numes

			Case ThisForm.poDataMgr.ChkRegister('SigMvCab',@laCampo3)
				If WExist('FORM_')
					MessageBox('Impossível Consultar Documento ... ' + Chr(13) + Chr(10) + ;
							   'Feche a Movimentação de Estoque',0+48,'')
				Else
					If fChecaAcesso([SIGOPIND], [ALTMOV])
						Do Form SigMvCab With crSigMvCcr.Dopes, crSigMvCcr.Numes, crSigMvCcr.Emps, .t., 3, ThisForm
						This.Enabled = .f.
					Else
						Do Form SigMvExp with crSigMvCcr.Dopes, 'C',crSigMvCcr.Numes, crSigMvCcr.Emps, .t.
					EndIf
				EndIf

			Case !Empty(crSigMvCcr.Emps) .And. !Empty(crSigMvCcr.Dopcs) .And. !Empty(crSigMvCcr.Numcs)
				If fChecaAcesso('C'+Padl(crSigOpOpe.NDopes, 8, '0')) 
					Do Form SigPgTit With crSigMvCcr.Dopcs, 'CONSULTAR', crSigMvCcr.Emps, Str(crSigMvCcr.Numcs, 6), ( CrSigOpOpe.RealPrevs = 2 )
				Else
					MessageBox('Usuário não tem acesso a Consultar Títulos!!!',0+16,'')
				EndIf

			Case ThisForm.poDataMgr.ChkRegister('SigCqChm',@laCampo2)
				Do Form SigCdLch with .t., Left( crSigMvCcr.VOpers, 3 ), Val( Alltrim( Substr( crSigMvCcr.Vopers, 4 ) ) )

			Case ThisForm.poDataMgr.ChkRegister('SigPrCtb',@laCampo1)
				Do Form SigCdPgb With crSigMvCcr.Emps, crSigMvCcr.Dopes, crSigMvCcr.Numes

			Case ThisForm.poDataMgr.ChkRegister('SigCdNec','EmpDnPs',CrSigMvCcr.emps+CrSigMvCcr.dopes+Str(CrSigMvCcr.numes,10))
				Do Form SigMvPdt With CrSigMvCcr.dopes,"C",CrSigMvCcr.numes,CrSigMvCcr.emps,.T.

		Endcase
	Endif
EndIf

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.Cmd_Consulta
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 86
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Local llSaida
If This.Enabled And Not (crSigMvCcr.Tipo = [P])
	ThisForm.DesabContainer()

	llSaida = .f.

	Select CrSigMvCcr

	If !Eof()

		pEmp   = crSigMvCcr.Emps
		pNoper = crSigMvCcr.Nopers
		
		ThisForm.poDataMgr.Requery('CrAltcc')
		Select crAltcc
		Go Top In crAltcc
		Locate For Opers = crSigMvCcr.Opers && 01/10/2014 - Richard - Não posicionava na perna correta, dependia de como vinha a select
		  
		If !fAcessoContab( Usuar, 'C', crAltcc.SGrupos )
			Messagebox( 'Acesso Negado...', 32, '' )
			llSaida=.T.
		Endif
		If !fAcessoContas( Usuar, crAltcc.SGrupos, 'C', crAltcc.SContas )
			Messagebox( 'Acesso Negado...', 32, '' )
			llSaida = .T.
		Endif
		If llSaida
			ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.Refresh
			ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.columns(1).SetFocus
			Return
		Endif
		Scatter Memvar Memo

		Do Form SigOpAcc With ThisForm,"CONSULTAR"
		ThisForm.Page.Saldo.Grd_Saldo.Refresh
	Endif
	ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.Refresh
	ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.columns(1).SetFocus
EndIf

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.Cmd_Alterar
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 87
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Local lnTpAltera, llSaida

If This.Enabled And Not (crSigMvCcr.Tipo = [P])
	ThisForm.DesabContainer()

	If !This.Visible
		Return
	Endif

	lnTpAltera = 0
	llSaida = .f.

	Select CrSigMvCcr
	If !Eof()
		pEmp   = crSigMvCcr.Emps
		pNoper = crSigMvCcr.Nopers
		lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.Requery('CrAltcc')
		Select crAltcc
		Go Top
		DO While !Eof() And !llSaida
			If CrAltcc.Opers # CrSigMvCcr.Opers
				lcComple = '( Contra Partida )'
			Else
				lcComple = ' '
			Endif
			If crAltcc.Autos
				Messagebox( 'Operação Inválida !! Movimentação Automática !'+lcComple,0+48,'')
				llSaida = .t.
			Endif
			If crAltcc.Concs and !llSaida
				Messagebox( 'Operação Inválida !! Movimentação Conciliada !'+lcComple,0+48,'')
				llSaida = .t.
			Endif
			Okdt = fVerificaBloqueio(crAltcc.datas, ThisForm.poDatamgr)=0
			If !okdt And !llSaida
				Messagebox('Periodo Bloqueado !!!',0+48,'')
				llsaida = .t.
			EndIf
			Okdt = fBloqueioPorPeriodo(crAltcc.datas, ThisForm.poDatamgr)=0
			If !okdt And !llSaida
				Messagebox('Periodo Bloqueado !!!',0+48,'')
				llsaida = .t.
			Endif
			If !fAcessoContab( Usuar, 'C', crAltcc.SGrupos ) and !llsaida
				Messagebox( 'Acesso ao Grupo Negado...'+lcComple, 32, '' )
				llsaida = .t.
			Endif
			If !fAcessoContas( Usuar, crAltcc.SGrupos, 'C', crAltcc.SContas ) and !llsaida
				Messagebox( 'Acesso a Conta Negado...'+lcComple, 32, '' )
				llsaida = .t.
			Endif
			If crAltcc.ContaPgs = 1 And crAltcc.ValPags # 0 And !llsaida
				messagebox('Operação Inválida !! Movimentação já foi Baixada!'+lcComple,0+48,'')
				llSaida = .t.
			ENDIF
			*** mena - 01/11/2016 - não altera se auditado - Joias aline ***
			If !EMPTY(CrAltcc.auditors) And !llsaida
				messagebox('Operação Inválida !! Movimentação Auditada!',0+48,'')
				llSaida = .t.
			EndIf
			Select CrAltcc
			Skip
		EndDo
		
		Select crAltcc
		Go Top
		Skip
		If crAltcc.ContaPgs = 1 And crAltcc.ValPags # 0 
			lnTpAltera = 1
		Endif
		If !llSaida
			Select crAltcc
			Go Top
			Scatter Memvar Memo
			Do Form SigOpAcc With ThisForm,"ALTERAR", lnTpAltera
			This.Parent.Cmb_Concilia.InteractiveChange
		Endif
		Select CrSigMvCcr
		Locate For Nopers = pNoper
		ThisForm.Page.Saldo.Grd_Saldo.Refresh
	Endif
	ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.Refresh
	ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.columns(1).SetFocus
EndIf

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.SigCcrimpressao.getEmps
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 102
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If !Empty( This.Value )
	m.loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext',ThisForm.poDataMgr.pnIdConn,'SigCdEmp','CrEmps','Cemps',This.Value,'Seleção',.T.)
	If .Not.m.loLista.plAchouRegistro
		m.loLista.mAddColuna('Cemps','XXX','Emp')
		m.loLista.mAddColuna('Razas','','Razão Social')
		m.loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	This.Parent.getEmps.Value  = CrEmps.Cemps
	This.Parent.getDEmps.Value = CrEmps.Razas
Else
	This.Parent.getDEmps.Value = []
Endif

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.SigCcrimpressao.get_cd_moeda
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 103
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If !Empty( This.Value )
	m.loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext',ThisForm.poDataMgr.pnIdConn,'SigCdMoe','crMoedas1','cmoes',This.Value,'Seleção',.T.)
	If .Not.m.loLista.plAchouRegistro
		m.loLista.mAddColuna('cmoes','XXXXXX','Código')
		m.loLista.mAddColuna('dmoes','','Descrição')
		m.loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	This.Parent.Get_cd_moeda.Value = crMoedas1.CMoes
	This.Parent.Get_ds_moeda.Value = crMoedas1.DMoes
Else
	This.Parent.Get_ds_moeda.Value = ''
Endif

With This.Parent.Check_RazaoCP
	.Value = Iif(Empty(This.Value),.F.,This.Parent.Check_RazaoCP.Value)
	.Enabled = Iif(Empty(This.Value),.F.,.T.)
	.Refresh
EndWith

With This.Parent.Check_RazaoDoc
	.Value = Iif(Empty(This.Value),.F.,This.Parent.Check_RazaoDoc.Value)
	.Enabled = Iif(Empty(This.Value),.F.,.T.)
	.Refresh
EndWith
ENDPROC

	If .Not.m.loLista.plAchouRegistro
		m.loLista.mAddColuna('dmoes','','Descrição')

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.SigCcrimpressao.getDEmps
* Classe: fwget (Base: textbox)
* Registro: 107
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Valid
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Valid
If !Empty( This.Value )
	m.loLista = CreateObject('fwbuscaext',ThisForm.poDataMgr.pnIdConn,'SigCdEmp','CrEmps','Razas',This.Value,'Seleção',.T.)
	If .Not.m.loLista.plAchouRegistro
		m.loLista.mAddColuna('Razas','','Razão Social')
		m.loLista.mAddColuna('Cemps','XXX','Emp')
		m.loLista.Show()
	EndIf
	This.Parent.getEmps.Value  = CrEmps.Cemps
	This.Parent.getDEmps.Value = CrEmps.Razas
Else
	This.Parent.getEmps.Value = []
Endif

ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE When
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE When
Return Empty(This.Parent.getEmps.Value)
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.Chk_Quitação
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 130
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Private lcEmpr, lnNope

If This.Enabled And Not (crSigMvCcr.Tipo = [P])
	ThisForm.DesabContainer()

	Select xTmpBaixa
	Delete All

	lcEmpr = crSigMvCcr.Emps
	lnNope = crSigMvCcr.Nopers

	lcQuery = [Select * From SigCdPbx Where ]+;
			  [Empos = ?lcEmpr And ]+[( Nopers = ?lnNope or Nopercs = ?lnNope) ]
	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'CrSigCdPbx') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (CrSigCdPbx)')
		Return 0
	EndIf

	Select CrSigCdPbx
	Scan
		Insert Into xTmpBaixa ( Emps, Dopes, Numes, Valos, Moedas ) ;
			Values ( crSigCdPbx.Emps, crSigCdPbx.Dopes, fGerMascara( crSigCdPbx.Numes ), crSigCdPbx.Valos, crSigCdPbx.Moedas )
	Endscan	

	Select xTmpBaixa
	If !Eof( 'xTmpBaixa' ) 
		Go Top
		This.Parent.grp_operacao.cmd_sair.Enabled = .f.
		thisForm.Page.Hist.Sigccrquitação.grade.refresh
		ThisForm.Page.Hist.Sigccrquitação.visible = .t.
		ThisForm.Page.Hist.Sigccrquitação.SetFocus
	Endif
EndIf

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.Cmb_Concilia
* Classe: combobox (Base: combobox)
* Registro: 131
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE InteractiveChange
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE InteractiveChange
DO Form SigPrRss With 'AUTO',Usuar,.t.

lcGSccr = crSigMvSlc.Grupos
lcCSccr = CrSigMvSlc.Contas
lcMSccr = CrSigMvSlc.Moedas

Create Cursor crMatrizes (Grupos c(10), Contas c(10), Moedas c(3))
Insert Into crMatrizes (Grupos, Contas, Moedas) Values (lcGSccr, lcCSccr, lcMSccr)

llDatai = ThisForm.Page.Saldo.Dt_inicial.Value
llDataf = ThisForm.Page.Saldo.Dt_Final.Value
llDatai = fDtoSQL(llDatai)
llDataf = fDtoSQL(llDataf,'23:59:59')

If Used('CrSigMvCcr')
	ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.RecordSource = ''
	Use In CrSigMvCcr
EndIf

If ThisForm.Matrizes
	If (crSigMvSlc.Matriz = [M])
		lcQuery = [Select IClis, Grupos From SigCdCli Where ContaMats = '] + CrSigMvSlc.Contas + [' And IClis <> '] + CrSigMvSlc.Contas + [']
	Else
		lcQuery = [Select IClis, Grupos From SigCdCli Where ContaMats = '] + CrSigMvSlc.ContaMats + [' And IClis <> '] + CrSigMvSlc.Contas + [']
	EndIf

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpMat') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpMat)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	Select crTmpMat
	Scan
		Insert Into crMatrizes (Grupos, Contas, Moedas) Values (crTmpMat.Grupos, crTmpMat.IClis, lcMSccr)
	EndScan
	Use In crTmpMat
EndIf

lcQuery = [Select ' ' as Tipo, a.Saldos as SaldosP, a.*, 0 as Seleciona From SigMvCcr a ] + ;
		   [Where a.GruConMoes = '] + Replicate(Chr(254), 23) + ['] 

If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpMccr') < 1)
	=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpMccr)')
	Return 0
EndIf

Select crMatrizes
Scan
	lcGCM = crMatrizes.Grupos + crMatrizes.Contas + crMatrizes.Moedas

    lcQuery = [Select 'R' as Tipo, a.Saldos as SaldosP, a.*, 0 as Seleciona From SigMvCcr a ] + ;
			   [Where a.GruConMoes = '] + lcGCM + [' And ] + ;
			          Iif(This.Value=[Só Conciliados], [a.DataConcs], [a.Datas]) + [ Between ?llDatai And ?llDataf ] + ;
 			   [Order By a.Grupos,a.Contas,a.Moedas,a.datas,a.nopers]
	
	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpMcc1') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpMcc1)')
		Return 0
	EndIf

	lcQuery = [Select 'P' as Tipo, b.Saldos as SaldosP, b.* From SigMvCpv b ] + ;
			   [Where b.GruConMoes = '] + lcGCM + [' And ] + ;
			         [b.Datas Between ?llDatai And ?llDataf]

	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'crTmpMcpv') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (crTmpMccr)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	
	Go Top In crTmpMccr

	Select CrTmpMcc1
	Scan
		Scatter Memvar Memo
		Insert Into crTmpMccr From Memvar
	EndScan
	
	Select CrTmpMcpv
	Scan
		Scatter Memvar Memo
		Insert Into crTmpMccr From Memvar
	EndScan
EndScan

Go Top In crTmpMccr

Select *, .f. as Prorrogados From CrTmpMccr Into Cursor TMccr ;
Order By Grupos,Contas,Moedas,datas,nopers

Select tMccr
Use Dbf('tMccr') In 0 Alias CrSigMvCcr Again
Use In tMccr

* Richard em 14/02/2012 - Para ordenar certo quando houver matriz e filiais processadas
If ThisForm.Matrizes
	lcMat = Padr(Iif(crSigMvSlc.Matriz = [M], CrSigMvSlc.Contas, CrSigMvSlc.ContaMats), 10)
	Update crSigMvCcr Set Contas = lcMat, GruConMoes = crSigMvCcr.Grupos + lcMat + crSigMvCcr.Moedas
EndIf

* Richard em 03/04/2012 - Para marcar títulos Prorrogados
Select crSigMvCcr
Scan
	lcQuery = [Select * From SigOpMvv Where EmpDopNcs = '] + crSigMvCcr.EmpDopNcs + [']
	If (ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery, 'LocalOpMvv') < 1)
		=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (LocalOpMvv)')
		Return 0
	EndIf
	If (Reccount([LocalOpMvv]) > 0)
		Replace Prorrogados With .t. In crSigMvCcr
	EndIf
EndScan

Select CrSigMvCcr
*!*	Index on GruConMoes + CidChaves Tag GruConMoe For Tipo = [R]
*!*	Index on GruConMoes + Dtos(DataConcs) + CidChaves Tag GruConMoec For Concs
*!*	Index on GruConMoes + CidChaves Tag GruConMoen For !Concs
*!*	Index on GruConMoes + CidChaves Tag GruConMoeP
Index on GruConMoes + Dtos(Datas) + CidChaves Tag GruConMoe For Tipo = [R]
Index on GruConMoes + Dtos(DataConcs) + CidChaves Tag GruConMoec For Concs
Index on GruConMoes + Dtos(Datas) + CidChaves Tag GruConMoen For !Concs
Index on GruConMoes + Dtos(Datas) + CidChaves Tag GruConMoeP

Set Order to GruConMoe
Go Bottom

If ThisForm.Matrizes
	Select CrSigMvCcr
	Go Top In CrSigMvCcr
	lnSal = 0
	Select CrSigMvCcr
	Scan
		If (CrSigMvCcr.Opers = [D])
			lnSal = lnSal + Abs(CrSigMvCcr.Valors)
		Else
			lnSal = lnSal - Abs(CrSigMvCcr.Valors)
		EndIf
		
		Replace Saldos With lnSal
	EndScan
EndIf

ThisForm.LockScreen = .t.

ThisForm.page.hist.cmd_consulta.Enabled = .t.
ThisForm.page.hist.cmd_alterar.Enabled = .t.
ThisForm.page.hist.cmd_excluir.Enabled = .t.
ThisForm.page.hist.anexa.Enabled = .t.
ThisForm.page.hist.chk_Auditoria.Enabled = .t.
ThisForm.page.hist.cmd_impressao.Enabled = .t.

Do Case
	Case This.Value = "Lançamentos Realizados"
		Set Order to GruConMoe Asce
		ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.RecordSource = "CrSigMvCcr"
		ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.col_data.ControlSource = "Datas"
		ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.col_saldo.ControlSource = "Saldos"

	Case This.Value = "Só Conciliados"
		Set Order to GruConMoec Asce
		ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.RecordSource = "CrSigMvCcr"
		ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.col_data.ControlSource = "DataConcs"
		ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.col_saldo.ControlSource = "Saldocs"

	Case This.Value = "Não Conciliados"
		Set Order to GruConMoen Asce
		ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.RecordSource = "CrSigMvCcr"
		ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.col_data.ControlSource = "Datas"
		ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.col_saldo.ControlSource = "Saldons"

	Case This.Value = "Realizado e Previsão"	
		Set Order to GruConMoeP
		ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.RecordSource = "CrSigMvCcr"
		ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.col_data.ControlSource = "Datas"
		ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.col_saldo.ControlSource = "SaldosP"
		
	Other
		ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.RecordSource = "xConta"
		ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.col_data.ControlSource = "Datas"
		ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.col_saldo.ControlSource = "Saldos"
		ThisForm.page.hist.cmd_consulta.Enabled = .f.
		ThisForm.page.hist.cmd_alterar.Enabled = .f.
		ThisForm.page.hist.cmd_excluir.Enabled = .f.
		ThisForm.page.hist.anexa.Enabled = .f.
		ThisForm.page.hist.chk_concilia.Enabled = .f.
		ThisForm.page.hist.chk_Auditoria.Enabled = .f.
		ThisForm.page.hist.cmd_impressao.Enabled = .f.
Endcase
ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.Col_Saldo.InputMask = '99,999,999,999.99'
ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.Col_Saldo.Text1.InputMask = '99,999,999,999.99'
ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.col_hist.ControlSource = "Hists"
ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.col_valor.ControlSource = "Valors"
ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.col_oper.ControlSource = "Alltrim(Iif(CrSigMvCcr.Opers=[D], ThisForm.TxtDeb, ThisForm.TxtCre))"
ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.col_emp.ControlSource = [iif(!Empty(CrSigMvCcr.EmpCCs),CrSigMvCcr.EmpCcs,CrSigMvCcr.Emps)]
ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.Column1.ControlSource = "Docus"
ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.Column2.ControlSource = "NFs"
ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.Column3.ControlSource = "Titulos"
ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.Column4.ControlSource = "Tipo"
ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.col_selec.ControlSource = "Seleciona"

If InList(Left(This.Value, 12), 'Saldo Diário', 'Realizado e ')
	lOrdenacao = 'GRUCONMOE'
	Do Case
		Case This.Value = "Saldo Diário - Todos"
			lOrdenacao = 'GRUCONMOE'

		Case This.Value = "Saldo Diário - Conciliados"
			lOrdenacao = 'GRUCONMOEC'

		Case This.Value = "Saldo Diário - Não Conciliados"
			lOrdenacao = 'GRUCONMOEN'
	Endcase
	Select CrSigMvCcr
	If (This.Value = "Realizado e Previsão"	)
		lOrdenacao = 'GRUCONMOEP'
		Set Order to ( lOrdenacao ) Ascending

		lnSal = 0
		Select CrSigMvCcr
		Scan
			If (CrSigMvCcr.Opers = [D])
				lnSal = lnSal + Abs(CrSigMvCcr.Valors)
			Else
				lnSal = lnSal - Abs(CrSigMvCcr.Valors)
			EndIf
			
			Replace SaldosP With lnSal
		EndScan
	Else
		Set Order to ( lOrdenacao ) Desc
	EndIf
	
	ThisForm.CarregaDia( lOrdenacao )	
EndIf

thisform.page.HIST.getTOTAL.Value = 0.00
thisform.page.HIST.getTOTAL.refresh

Select CrSigMvCcr
Go Bottom
ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.refresh
ThisForm.page.hist.grd_lancamento.setfocus
ThisForm.LockScreen = .f.

ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.Chk_Auditoria
* Classe: checkbox (Base: checkbox)
* Registro: 145
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Local lcOldOrdem, lcOldKey1, lcOldKey2, lnOldRecNo
Private llData

If This.Enabled And Not (crSigMvCcr.Tipo = [P])
	ThisForm.DesabContainer()

	If !This.Visible
		Return
	EndIf

	Select CrSigCqOch
	Zap

	Select CrSigMvCcr
	If !Eof() And !Bof()
		If Not Empty(Auditors) And (Auditors <> Usuar Or crSigCdPac.CanAudits = 1) And Not fChecaAcesso('SIGOPIND', 'AUDMASTER')
			Messagebox( 'Auditoria Não Pode Ser Desfeita Por Este Usuário!!!' + Chr(13) + ;
						'Ela Foi Executada Por ' + Alltrim(Auditors) +'!!!', 48, '' )
			This.Value = .t.
			ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.Refresh
			ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.columns(1).SetFocus
			Return
		Endif

		If fChecaAcesso( "SIGOPIND", "CONCILIA" ) And CrSigCdCli.Concilias = 1
			ThisForm.Page.Hist.Chk_Concilia.Value = CrSigMvCcr.Concs
		EndIf
		
		llData = Evaluate([{^]+TransForm((DtoS(Date())+'235959'),'@R 9999-99-99 99:99:99')+[}])
		llTval = This.Value
		
*!*			lcQuery = [Select DtAudits, Auditors, Cidchaves From SigMvCcr Where GruConMoes = ']+crSigMvCcr.Grupos+crSigMvCcr.Contas+crSigMvCcr.Moedas+[' ]+;
*!*						[And Nopers = ]+Str(crSigMvCcr.Nopers)+[ And Opers = ']+CrSigMvCcr.Opers+[']
		lcCta = Iif(Empty(Alltrim(ThisForm.Page.Hist.getCtaCob.Value)), crSigMvCcr.Contas, Padr(ThisForm.Page.Hist.getCtaCob.Value, 10))
		
		lcQuery = [Select DtAudits, Auditors, Cidchaves From SigMvCcr Where GruConMoes = ']+crSigMvCcr.Grupos + lcCta + crSigMvCcr.Moedas+[' ]+;
					[And Nopers = ]+Str(crSigMvCcr.Nopers)+[ And Opers = ']+CrSigMvCcr.Opers+[']
		lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'TmpMccr2')
		If lnErro > 0
			If llTval
				If Not Empty(TmpMccr2.Auditors)
					Messagebox( 'Lançamento já Auditado Por ' + Alltrim(Auditors) +'!!!', 48, '' )
					This.Value = .t.
					ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.Refresh
					ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.columns(1).SetFocus
					Return
				EndIf
			Else
				If Empty(TmpMccr2.Auditors)
					Messagebox( 'Lançamento não Auditado !!!', 48, '' )
					This.Value = .t.
					ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.Refresh
					ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.columns(1).SetFocus
					Return
				EndIf
			Endif
					
			If llTval
				lctipo   = 'Auditado'
				lcUpdate = [Update SigMvCcr Set DtAudits = ?llData, Auditors = ']+Usuar+[ ' Where Cidchaves = ']+TmpMccr2.CidChaves+[']
			Else
				lctipo   = 'DesAuditado'
				llData = .Null.
				lcUpdate = [Update SigMvCcr Set DtAudits = ?llData, Auditors = '          ' Where Cidchaves = ']+TmpMccr2.CidChaves+[']
			Endif
		
			Insert into CrSigCqOch (Nopers, Datars, TpLancs, Datas, Usuars, CidChaves,DtLancs ) Values ;
			(CrSigMvCcr.Nopers, Datetime(), lcTipo, llData, Usuar, fUniqueIds(), CrSigMvCcr.Datas )
			
			If CrSigCdPac.ObsAudts = 1

				DO Form SigOpOca With Thisform.datasessionid, ThisForm, llTval, ThisForm.page.hist.Chk_Auditoria.Top

			Else

				If ThisForm.PodataMgr.update('CrSigCqOch')
					lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
				Else
					lnErro = 0
				Endif
				If lnErro > 0
					ThisForm.poDataMgr.Commit()
					
					Select CrSigMvCcr
					If llTval
						Replace DtAudits With Datetime(), ;
						        Auditors With Usuar
					Else
						Replace DtAudits With {} ,;
						        Auditors With " "
					Endif
				EndIf
				Thisform.Page.Hist.Get_DtAudits.Refresh
				ThisForm.Page.Hist.Get_Auditors.Refresh
			EndIf
		Endif
	Endif
EndIf


ENDPROC

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE LostFocus
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE LostFocus
If This.Enabled
	Thisform.Page.Hist.Get_DtAudits.Refresh
	ThisForm.Page.Hist.Get_Auditors.Refresh
	ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.Refresh
	ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.columns(1).SetFocus
EndIf
ENDPROC

================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.Chk_Concilia
* Classe: checkbox (Base: checkbox)
* Registro: 154
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
Local lcOldOrdem, lcOldKey1, lcOldKey2, lnOldRecNo, llChkConc, Ok, lcQuery, pValue
Private llData, llConc

If This.Enabled And Not (crSigMvCcr.Tipo = [P])
	pValue = This.Value

	thisform.reg = RECNO('crSigmvccr')	&& mena - 28/06/2017 - salva linha atual para não carregar a grade no hist.Activate

	ThisForm.DesabContainer()

	If !This.Visible
		Return
	Endif

	llTransOk1 = .t.
	ok=.t.

	Select CrSigMvCcr
	SCAN for seleciona = 1
		&&If !Eof() And !Bof()
		If Concs .and. UsuConcs # Usuar .and. !fChecaAcesso('SIGOPIND','CONCMASTER')
			Messagebox( 'Conciliação Não Pode Ser Desfeita Por Este Usuário!!!' + Chr(13) + ;
						'Ela Foi Executada Por ' + Alltrim(UsuConcs) +'!!!', 48, '' )
			This.Value = .t.
			ok=.f.
		Endif

		If Empty( ThisForm.Page.Hist.Get_Data.Value ) and ok
			Messagebox( 'Data da Conciliação Não Informada...' ,  48, '' )
			ThisForm.Page.Hist.Get_Data.SetFocus
			ok=.f.
		EndIf
		
		lcSql = [Select * From SigSyBlq ]
		If ThisForm.Podatamgr.Sqlexecute(lcSql,'TmpBloq') < 1
			=MessageBox('Favor Reinicializar o Processo!!!', 16, 'Falha na Conexão (SigSyBlq)')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		If TmpBloq.ChkConcs = 1 And fVerificaBloqueio(ThisForm.Page.Hist.Get_Data.Value, ThisForm.poDatamgr) # 0
			Messagebox('Periodo Bloqueado !!!',0+48,'')
			Return 0
		EndIf
		
		If TmpBloq.ChkConcs = 1 And fBloqueioPorPeriodo(ThisForm.Page.Hist.Get_Data.Value, ThisForm.poDatamgr) # 0
			Messagebox('Periodo Bloqueado !!!',0+48,'')
			Return 0
		Endif
		Select CrSigMvCcr

		If Not CrSigMvCcr.Concs .And. Ttod(ThisForm.Page.Hist.Get_Data.Value) < Ttod(CrSigMvCcr.Datas) and ok And TmpBloq.ChkConcs <> 2
			Messagebox( 'A Data da Conciliação Não Pode Ser Menor Que a Do Lançamento!!!', 48, '' )
			This.Value = .f.
			ok=.f.
		EndIf
		
		If CrSigMvCcr.Concs and ok
			If MessageBox('Confirma a Desconciliação do Lançamento?', 4+32, '') # 6
				This.Value = .t.
				ok=.f.
			Endif
		Endif
		
		If !Ok
*!*					ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.Refresh
*!*					ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.columns(1).SetFocus
			Select CrSigMvCcr
			loop	&&	Mena - 29/11/2017 - conciliada todas marcadas &&Return
		EndIf
		
		Select CrSigCqOch
		Zap

		Thisform.LockScreen = .T.	
		Select CrSigMvCcr
		
		Ok     = .t.
		llConc = pValue
		llData = ThisForm.Page.Hist.Get_Data.Value
		llData = Evaluate([{^]+TransForm((DtoS(Max(llData,CtoD('01/01/1900')))+'235959'),'@R 9999-99-99 99:99:99')+[}])

		lcQuery = [Select DataConcs, Concs, UsuConcs, Cidchaves From SigMvCcr Where CidChaves = ']+CrSigMvCcr.CidChaves+[']

		If ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcQuery,'TmpMccr') > 0 And Reccount('TmpMccr') > 0
			Select TmpMccr
			If pValue
				If TmpMccr.Concs
					Messagebox( 'Lançamento já conciliado Por ' + Alltrim(TmpMccr.UsuConcs) +'!!!', 48, '' )
					This.Value = .t.
					ok=.f.
				Endif
			Else
				If Not TmpMccr.Concs
					Messagebox( 'Lançamento já desconciliado !!!', 48, '' )
					This.Value = .t.
					ok=.f.
				Endif		
			Endif

			If !Ok
				Select CrSigMvCcr
				loop	&&	Mena - 29/11/2017 - conciliada todas marcadas &&Return
			Endif

			If pValue
				lcTipo = 'Conciliado'
				lcUpdate = [Update SigMvCcr Set DataConcs = ?llData, Concs = ?llConc , UsuConcs = ']+Usuar+[ ' Where CidChaves = ']+TmpMccr.CidChaves+[']
			Else
				lcTipo = 'DesConciliado'
				llData = .Null.
				lcUpdate = [Update SigMvCcr Set DataConcs = ?llData, Concs = ?llConc , UsuConcs = ' ' Where CidChaves = ']+TmpMccr.CidChaves+[']
			EndIf

			Insert into CrSigCqOch (Nopers, Datars, TpLancs, Datas, Usuars, CidChaves ) Values ;
			(CrSigMvCcr.Nopers, Datetime(), lcTipo, llData, Usuar, fUniqueIds() )
			
			If ThisForm.PodataMgr.update('CrSigCqOch')
				lnErro = ThisForm.poDataMgr.SqlExecute(lcUpdate,'')
			Else
				lnErro = 0
			Endif
			If lnErro > 0

				=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, llData, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas, ThisForm.Podatamgr  )
				=fRecalculaS( CrSigMvCcr.Grupos, CrSigMvCcr.Contas, CrSigMvCcr.Datas, CrSigMvCcr.Moedas, ThisForm.Podatamgr  )

				If Not fRecalculaS( .t., ThisForm.Podatamgr, .f., .f., .f., .f.,.f.,.t. )
					Thisform.Podatamgr.Rollback()
					Select CrSigMvCcr
					loop
				Endif

				If pValue
					Replace DataConcs With ThisForm.Page.Hist.Get_Data.Value, ;
					        UsuConcs  With Usuar In CrSigMvCcr
				Else
					Replace DataConcs With Ctod("  /  /  ") ,;
					        UsuConcs  With " " in CrSigMvCcr
				Endif
				Replace Concs With pValue in CrSigMvCcr
				
				ThisForm.poDataMgr.Commit()

			Else
				ThisForm.poDataMgr.RollBack()
			Endif
		Else
			ThisForm.poDataMgr.RollBack()
		Endif
		Select CrSigMvCcr	&& Mena - 29/11/2017 - concilia todas marcadas
	&&Endif
	ENDSCAN
	Thisform.page.hist.get3.refresh
	Thisform.LockScreen = .F.	
*!*		Thisform.LockScreen = .F.	
*!*		ThisForm.page.hist.Chk_Concilia.Refresh
*!*		ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.Refresh
*!*		ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.SetFocus
EndIf

ENDPROC


================================================================================
* OBJETO: SIGOPIND.Page.HIST.Chk_Follow
* Classe: commandbutton (Base: commandbutton)
* Registro: 155
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
* PROCEDURE Click
------------------------------------------------------------
PROCEDURE Click
If This.Enabled And Not (crSigMvCcr.Tipo = [P])
	lcSql = [Select * From SigCqOch where Nopers = ]+Str(CrSigMvCcr.Nopers)
	Thisform.PodataMgr.SqlExecute(lcSql,'lancto')
	Select Lancto
	If Reccount() = 0
		MessageBox('Nenhum lançamento de Follow-up Conciliado/Auditado!!!',0+32,'')
	Else
		ThisForm.DesabContainer()
		Do Form SigOpOch With Thisform,crSigMvCcr.Nopers,CrSigMvCcr.Opers
	EndIf
	Select CrSigMvCcr
	ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.Refresh
	ThisForm.Page.Hist.Grd_Lancamento.Columns(1).SetFocus
EndIf

ENDPROC
